Routine clinical imaging of the retina using optical coherence tomography (OCT) is performed with large slice spacing, resulting in highly anisotropic images and a sparsely scanned retina.
Generating a Paracosm for Training-Free Zero-Shot Composed Image Retrieval
Authors: Tong Wang, Yunhan Zhao, Shu Kong
First: 2026-01-31T16:42:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-11T15:40:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) is the task of retrieving a target image from a database using a multimodal query, which consists of a reference image and a modification text. The text specifies how to alter the reference image to form a ''mental image'', based on which CIR should find the target image in the database. The fundamental challenge of CIR is that this ''mental image'' is not physically available and is only implicitly defined by the query. The contemporary literature pursues zero-shot methods and uses a Large Multimodal Model (LMM) to generate a textual description for a given multimodal query, and then employs a Vision-Language Model (VLM) for textual-visual matching to search for the target image. In contrast, we address CIR from first principles by directly generating the ''mental image'' for more accurate matching. Particularly, we prompt an LMM to generate a ''mental image'' for a given multimodal query and propose to use this ''mental image'' to search for the target image. As the ''mental image'' has a synthetic-to-real domain gap with real images, we also generate a synthetic counterpart for each real image in the database to facilitate matching. In this sense, our method uses LMM to construct a ``paracosm'', where it matches the multimodal query and database images. Hence, we call this method Paracosm. Notably, Paracosm is a training-free zero-shot CIR method. It significantly outperforms existing zero-shot methods on challenging benchmarks, achieving state-of-the-art performance for zero-shot CIR.
中文标题/摘要
标题：为无监督零样本合成图像检索生成平行宇宙
合成图像检索（CIR）任务是通过由参考图像和修改文本组成的多模态查询，从数据库中检索目标图像。文本指定了如何修改参考图像以形成“心理图像”，CIR应基于此心理图像在数据库中查找目标图像。CIR的核心挑战在于该“心理图像”不存在于物理世界，仅由查询隐式定义。当前文献追求零样本方法，利用大型多模态模型（LMM）为给定多模态查询生成文本描述，再通过视觉-语言模型（VLM）进行文本-视觉匹配以搜索目标图像。相反，我们从第一性原理出发，直接生成“心理图像”以实现更精确的匹配。具体而言，我们提示LMM为给定多模态查询生成“心理图像”，并提出用该“心理图像”搜索目标图像。由于“心理图像”与真实图像存在合成到真实域的差距，我们还为数据库中的每张真实图像生成其合成对应物以促进匹配。在此意义上，我们的方法利用LMM构建一个“平行宇宙”，其中匹配多模态查询与数据库图像。因此，我们称该方法为Paracosm。值得注意的是，Paracosm是一种无训练的零样本CIR方法，在具有挑战性的基准测试中显著优于现有零样本方法，实现了零样本CIR的最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of retrieving target images using implicit mental images defined by multimodal queries, this work introduces Paracosm, a training-free zero-shot composed image retrieval method. Instead of relying on vision-language models to match textual descriptions with images, it directly prompts a large multimodal model to generate a synthetic 'mental image' from the query and uses this generated image to search for the target in a database. To bridge the synthetic-to-real domain gap, it also synthesizes a counterpart for each real image in the database, enabling more accurate matching within a constructed paracosm. Experimental results show Paracosm significantly outperforms existing zero-shot methods on benchmark datasets, achieving state-of-the-art performance without any training.
为解决多模态查询中隐式‘心理图像’难以匹配的问题，本文提出Paracosm方法，一种无需训练的零样本组合图像检索方案。该方法直接利用大模型生成查询对应的合成‘心理图像’，并以此图像在数据库中进行检索；为弥合合成图像与真实图像的域差距，对数据库中的每张真实图像也生成其合成对应物，构建一个‘平行宇宙’以提升匹配精度。实验表明，Paracosm在多个基准数据集上显著优于现有零样本方法，达到当前最优性能，且完全无需训练。
S2D: Sparse to Dense Lifting for 3D Reconstruction with Minimal Inputs
Authors: Yuzhou Ji, Qijian Tian, He Zhu, Xiaoqi Jiang, Guangzhi Cao, Lizhuang Ma, Yuan Xie, Xin Tan
First: 2026-03-11T15:37:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-11T15:37:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Explicit 3D representations have already become an essential medium for 3D simulation and understanding. However, the most commonly used point cloud and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) each suffer from non-photorealistic rendering and significant degradation under sparse inputs. In this paper, we introduce Sparse to Dense lifting (S2D), a novel pipeline that bridges the two representations and achieves high-quality 3DGS reconstruction with minimal inputs. Specifically, the S2D lifting is two-fold. We first present an efficient one-step diffusion model that lifts sparse point cloud for high-fidelity image artifact fixing. Meanwhile, to reconstruct 3D consistent scenes, we also design a corresponding reconstruction strategy with random sample drop and weighted gradient for robust model fitting from sparse input views to dense novel views. Extensive experiments show that S2D achieves the best consistency in generating novel view guidance and first-tier sparse view reconstruction quality under different input sparsity. By reconstructing stable scenes with the least possible captures among existing methods, S2D enables minimal input requirements for 3DGS applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：S2D：稀疏到稠密提升用于最小输入下的3D重建
显式3D表示已成为3D仿真与理解的重要媒介。然而，最常用的点云和3D高斯光栅（3DGS）均存在非真实感渲染及在稀疏输入下显著退化的问题。本文提出稀疏到稠密提升（S2D），一种连接两种表示并以最小输入实现高质量3DGS重建的新流程。具体而言，S2D提升包含两部分：首先，我们提出一种高效的单步扩散模型，用于提升稀疏点云以修复高保真图像伪影；同时，为重建3D一致场景，我们设计了相应的重建策略，通过随机采样丢弃与加权梯度，从稀疏输入视角稳健拟合至稠密新视角。大量实验表明，S2D在不同输入稀疏度下生成新视角引导和首层稀疏视角重建质量方面表现最优。通过在现有方法中以最少捕获实现稳定场景重建，S2D使3DGS应用具备最小输入要求。
Summary / 总结
Explicit 3D representations have already become an essential medium for 3D simulation and understanding.
Modelling Language using Large Language Models
Authors: Jumbly Grindrod
First: 2024-04-15T08:40:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-11T15:36:40+00:00
Comments: Philosophical Studies (2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper argues that large language models have a valuable scientific role to play in serving as scientific models of public languages. Linguistic study should not only be concerned with the cognitive processes behind linguistic competence, but also with language understood as an external, social entity. Once this is recognized, the value of large language models as scientific models becomes clear. This paper defends the position against a number of arguments to the effect that language models provide no linguistic insight. Building upon Weisberg's (2007) notion of a model construal, it is then argued that recent work in computational linguistics to better understand the inner workings of large language models can be used to develop a model construal for large language models as models of a language.
中文标题/摘要

A Hybrid Knowledge-Grounded Framework for Safety and Traceability in Prescription Verification
Authors: Yichi Zhu, Kan Ling, Xu Liu, Hengrun Zhang, Huiqun Yu, Guisheng Fan
First: 2026-03-11T15:35:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-11T15:35:55+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 7 figures.Framework for safe prescription auditing and hybrid knowledge-grounded reasoning
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Medication errors pose a significant threat to patient safety, making pharmacist verification (PV) a critical, yet heavily burdened, final safeguard. The direct application of Large Language Models (LLMs) to this zero-tolerance domain is untenable due to their inherent factual unreliability, lack of traceability, and weakness in complex reasoning. To address these challenges, we introduce PharmGraph-Auditor, a novel system designed for safe and evidence-grounded prescription auditing. The core of our system is a trustworthy Hybrid Pharmaceutical Knowledge Base (HPKB), implemented under the Virtual Knowledge Graph (VKG) paradigm. This architecture strategically unifies a relational component for set constraint satisfaction and a graph component for topological reasoning via a rigorous mapping layer. To construct this HPKB, we propose the Iterative Schema Refinement (ISR) algorithm, a framework that enables the co-evolution of both graph and relational schemas from medical texts. For auditing, we introduce the KB-grounded Chain of Verification (CoV), a new reasoning paradigm that transforms the LLM from an unreliable generator into a transparent reasoning engine. CoV decomposes the audit task into a sequence of verifiable queries against the HPKB, generating hybrid query plans to retrieve evidence from the most appropriate data store. Experimental results demonstrate robust knowledge extraction capabilities and show promises of using PharmGraph-Auditor to enable pharmacists to achieve safer and faster prescription verification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种用于处方核验的安全与可追溯性混合知识框架
药物错误对患者安全构成重大威胁，药剂师核验（PV）是至关重要的最终防线，但负担沉重。由于大语言模型（LLMs）固有的事实不可靠性、缺乏可追溯性及复杂推理能力薄弱，直接应用于零容忍领域不可行。为解决这些问题，我们提出了PharmGraph-Auditor系统，旨在实现安全且证据基础的处方审核。该系统核心是一个可信的混合药学知识库（HPKB），基于虚拟知识图谱（VKG）范式构建。该架构通过严谨的映射层，战略性地统一了用于集合约束满足的关系组件与用于拓扑推理的图组件。为构建HPKB，我们提出迭代模式精炼（ISR）算法，该框架可从医学文本中协同演化图与关系模式。在审核方面，我们引入基于知识库的验证链（CoV），一种新的推理范式，将LLM从不可靠的生成器转变为透明的推理引擎。CoV将审核任务分解为一系列可验证的HPKB查询，生成混合查询计划以从最合适的存储中提取证据。实验结果表明，该系统具备强大的知识提取能力，并展示了PharmGraph-Auditor助力药剂师实现更安全、更快速处方核验的潜力。

A gripper for flap separation and opening of sealed bags
Authors: Sergi Foix, Jaume Oriol, Carme Torras, Júlia Borràs
First: 2026-03-11T15:34:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-11T15:34:22+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, Accepted at the 2026 IEEE International Conference on Robotics & Automation (ICRA2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Separating thin, flexible layers that must be individually grasped is a common but challenging manipulation primitive for most off-the-shelf grippers. A prominent example arises in clinical settings: the opening of sterile flat pouches for the preparation of the operating room, where the first step is to separate and grasp the flaps. We present a novel gripper design and opening strategy that enables reliable flap separation and robust seal opening. This capability addresses a high-volume repetitive hospital procedure in which nurses manually open up to 240 bags per shift, a physically demanding task linked to musculoskeletal injuries. Our design combines an active dented-roller fingertip with compliant fingers that exploit environmental constraints to robustly grasp thin flexible flaps. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed gripper reliably grasps and separates sealed bag flaps and other thin-layered materials from the hospital, the most sensitive variable affecting performance being the normal force applied. When two copies of the gripper grasp both flaps, the system withstands the forces needed to open the seals robustly. To our knowledge, this is one of the first demonstrations of robotic assistance to automate this repetitive, low-value, but critical hospital task.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于分离和开启密封袋的夹持器
分离必须逐个抓取的薄而柔韧层，是大多数现成夹持器常见的但具有挑战性的操作基本单元。一个典型例子出现在临床环境中：为手术室准备无菌扁平袋时，第一步是分离并夹持袋的翻盖。我们提出了一种新颖的夹持器设计和开启策略，可实现可靠翻盖分离和坚固密封开启。该能力解决了医院中高频率重复性操作——护士每班次手动开启多达240个袋子，这是一项与肌肉骨骼损伤相关的体力劳动。我们的设计结合了主动凹槽滚轮指尖与顺应性手指，利用环境约束可靠地抓取薄柔性翻盖。实验表明，所提出的夹持器能可靠抓取并分离密封袋翻盖及其他薄层材料，影响性能的最关键变量是施加的法向力。当两个夹持器同时抓取两侧翻盖时，系统可承受开启密封所需的力，实现稳健操作。据我们所知，这是首次实现机器人辅助自动化此类重复、低价值但关键的医院任务。

Motivated by the need to automate the physically taxing task of opening sterile sealed bags in hospitals—where nurses manually handle up to 240 bags per shift—this paper introduces a novel gripper design featuring an active dented-roller fingertip and compliant fingers that exploit environmental constraints to reliably grasp and separate thin, flexible flaps. Experimental results show the gripper successfully opens sealed bags and other thin-layered materials with high consistency, with normal force being the most sensitive performance variable; when two grippers cooperate to grasp both flaps, the system can withstand the forces required to open seals robustly, marking one of the first demonstrations of robotic assistance for this critical but repetitive hospital procedure.
为解决医院中护士每日手动开启多达240个密封袋的高负担、易致肌肉骨骼损伤的重复性任务，本文提出一种新型夹爪设计，采用主动凹槽滚轮指尖与柔性指头，利用环境约束可靠抓取并分离薄柔性层。实验表明，该夹爪能稳定开启密封袋及其他薄层材料，其中法向力是影响性能最敏感的变量；当两个夹爪协同抓取两侧封口时，系统可承受开启密封所需的力，这是首次实现此类关键但低价值医院任务的机器人辅助自动化。
TEAR: Temporal-aware Automated Red-teaming for Text-to-Video Models
Authors: Jiaming He, Guanyu Hou, Hongwei Li, Zhicong Huang, Kangjie Chen, Yi Yu, Wenbo Jiang, Guowen Xu, Tianwei Zhang
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2025-11-26T07:58:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-11T15:33:42+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-to-Video (T2V) models are capable of synthesizing high-quality, temporally coherent dynamic video content, but the diverse generation also inherently introduces critical safety challenges. Existing safety evaluation methods,which focus on static image and text generation, are insufficient to capture the complex temporal dynamics in video generation. To address this, we propose a TEmporal-aware Automated Red-teaming framework, named TEAR, an automated framework designed to uncover safety risks specifically linked to the dynamic temporal sequencing of T2V models. TEAR employs a temporal-aware test generator optimized via a two-stage approach: initial generator training and temporal-aware online preference learning, to craft textually innocuous prompts that exploit temporal dynamics to elicit policy-violating video output. And a refine model is adopted to improve the prompt stealthiness and adversarial effectiveness cyclically. Extensive experimental evaluation demonstrates the effectiveness of TEAR across open-source and commercial T2V systems with over 80% attack success rate, a significant boost from prior best result of 57%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TEAR：面向文本到视频模型的时序感知自动化红队攻击框架
文本到视频（T2V）模型能够合成高质量、时序连贯的动态视频内容，但其多样化的生成过程也内在地引入了关键的安全挑战。现有安全评估方法主要针对静态图像和文本生成，难以捕捉视频生成中的复杂时序动态。为此，我们提出了一种时序感知的自动化红队攻击框架TEAR，专门用于揭示T2V模型动态时序序列相关的安全风险。TEAR采用两阶段优化的时序感知测试生成器：初始生成器训练与时序感知在线偏好学习，以生成在文本上无害但能利用时序动态诱导违规视频输出的提示；并采用改进模型循环提升提示的隐蔽性与对抗有效性。大量实验表明，TEAR在开源与商业T2V系统上攻击成功率超过80%，较此前最佳结果（57%）显著提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the safety risks inherent in the temporal dynamics of text-to-video models, TEAR introduces an automated red-teaming framework designed to uncover policy-violating outputs by exploiting video generation sequences. It employs a two-stage temporal-aware test generator trained to produce textually benign prompts that trigger adversarial video content, followed by a refinement loop to enhance stealth and effectiveness. Experiments across open-source and commercial T2V systems show TEAR achieves over 80% attack success rate, significantly outperforming prior methods at 57%.
为应对文本到视频模型在时间序列生成中固有的安全风险，TEAR提出了一种面向时间动态的自动化红队框架，通过构建隐蔽提示语来触发违规视频输出。该框架采用两阶段时间感知测试生成器，结合初始训练与在线偏好学习进行优化，并辅以循环精炼模型提升攻击隐蔽性与有效性。实验表明，TEAR在多个开源与商业视频生成系统上攻击成功率超过80%，显著优于此前最高纪录57%。
Dynamics-Predictive Sampling for Active RL Finetuning of Large Reasoning Models
Authors: Yixiu Mao, Yun Qu, Qi Wang, Heming Zou, Xiangyang Ji
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-03-11T15:31:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-11T15:31:14+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) finetuning has become a key technique for enhancing the reasoning abilities of large language models (LLMs). However, its effectiveness critically depends on the selection of training data. Recent advances underscore the importance of online prompt selection methods, which typically concentrate training on partially solved or moderately challenging examples under the current policy, thereby yielding more effective model updates. While significantly accelerating RL finetuning in terms of training steps, they also incur substantial computational overhead by requiring extensive LLM rollouts over large candidate batches to identify informative samples, an expense that can outweigh the finetuning process itself. To address this challenge, this work proposes Dynamics-Predictive Sampling (DPS), which online predicts and selects informative prompts by inferring their learning dynamics prior to costly rollouts. Specifically, we introduce a new perspective by modeling each prompt's solving progress during RL finetuning as a dynamical system, where the extent of solving is represented as the state and the transition is characterized by a hidden Markov model. Using historical rollout reward signals, we perform online Bayesian inference to estimate evolving state distributions, and the inference outcome provides a predictive prior for efficient prompt selection without rollout-intensive filtering. Empirical results across diverse reasoning tasks, including mathematics, planning, and visual geometry, demonstrate that DPS substantially reduces redundant rollouts, accelerates the training process, and achieves superior reasoning performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于动态预测采样的主动强化学习微调大推理模型
强化学习（RL）微调已成为增强大语言模型（LLMs）推理能力的关键技术。然而，其效果高度依赖于训练数据的选择。近期进展强调了在线提示选择方法的重要性，这类方法通常聚焦于当前策略下部分解决或中等难度的示例，从而实现更有效的模型更新。虽然在训练步数上显著加速了RL微调，但它们也因需要在大规模候选批次上进行大量LLM rollout以识别信息性样本，而产生高昂的计算开销，该开销有时甚至超过微调过程本身。为解决这一挑战，本文提出动态预测采样（DPS），通过在昂贵的rollout之前预测并选择信息性提示，推断其学习动态。具体而言，我们引入新视角，将每个提示在RL微调中的求解进展建模为一个动态系统，其中求解程度作为状态，转移由隐马尔可夫模型表征。利用历史rollout奖励信号，我们进行在线贝叶斯推断以估计演化状态分布，推断结果为高效提示选择提供预测先验，无需进行密集的rollout筛选。在包括数学、规划和视觉几何在内的多种推理任务上的实验证明，DPS显著减少了冗余rollout，加速了训练过程，并实现了更优的推理性能。

This paper addresses the high computational cost of online prompt selection in reinforcement learning finetuning of large reasoning models, where current methods require extensive LLM rollouts to identify informative samples. The authors propose Dynamics-Predictive Sampling (DPS), which models each prompt’s solving progress as a dynamical system with hidden Markov transitions, using historical reward signals to perform online Bayesian inference and predict informative prompts without costly rollouts. Experiments across mathematics, planning, and visual geometry tasks show DPS significantly reduces redundant rollouts, accelerates training, and improves reasoning performance compared to baseline methods.
该论文针对强化学习微调大语言模型时在线提示选择所引发的高计算开销问题，提出Dynamics-Predictive Sampling（DPS）方法，通过将每个提示的求解进度建模为具有隐马尔可夫转移的动态系统，并利用历史奖励信号进行在线贝叶斯推断，预测高价值提示以避免昂贵的rollout筛选。在数学、规划和视觉几何等任务上的实验表明，DPS显著减少冗余rollout、加速训练并提升推理性能。
Kernel Tests of Equivalence
Authors: Xing Liu, Axel Gandy
First: 2026-03-11T15:30:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-11T15:30:57+00:00
Comments: 29 pages; 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose novel kernel-based tests for assessing the equivalence between distributions. Traditional goodness-of-fit testing is inappropriate for concluding the absence of distributional differences, because failure to reject the null hypothesis may simply be a result of lack of test power, also known as the Type-II error. This motivates \emph{equivalence testing}, which aims to assess the \emph{absence} of a statistically meaningful effect under controlled error rates. However, existing equivalence tests are either limited to parametric distributions or focus only on specific moments rather than the full distribution. We address these limitations using two kernel-based statistical discrepancies: the \emph{kernel Stein discrepancy} and the \emph{Maximum Mean Discrepancy}. The null hypothesis of our proposed tests assumes the candidate distribution differs from the nominal distribution by at least a pre-defined margin, which is measured by these discrepancies. We propose two approaches for computing the critical values of the tests, one using an asymptotic normality approximation, and another based on bootstrapping. Numerical experiments are conducted to assess the performance of these tests.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional goodness-of-fit tests in detecting distributional equivalence due to low statistical power, this paper introduces kernel-based equivalence tests that formally assess the absence of meaningful distributional differences under controlled Type-I and Type-II error rates. The method leverages two kernel discrepancies — the kernel Stein discrepancy and the Maximum Mean Discrepancy — to quantify deviation between a candidate and nominal distribution, with the null hypothesis positing that the difference exceeds a predefined margin. Critical values are computed via asymptotic normality or bootstrapping, and numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed tests effectively control error rates while maintaining high power to detect true equivalence.
鉴于传统拟合优度检验在检测分布等价性时因检验效能不足而易出现假阴性，本文提出基于核方法的等价性检验，利用核Stein离散度与最大均值差异评估两分布差异是否在预设阈值内。零假设设定为分布间存在至少预设差异，从而控制二类错误。提出两种临界值计算方法：渐近正态近似与自助法。实验结果表明，所提方法能有效控制一类错误，同时在多种场景下具备高检验效能以识别真实分布差异。
STREAM-VAE: Dual-Path Routing for Slow and Fast Dynamics in Vehicle Telemetry Anomaly Detection
Authors: Kadir-Kaan Özer, René Ebeling, Markus Enzweiler
First: 2025-11-19T10:58:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-11T15:30:49+00:00
Comments: Accepted to appear in the 2026 IEEE Intelligent Vehicles Symposium (IV 2026), Detroit, MI, USA, June 22-25, 2026. 8 Pages, 4 Figures, 4 Tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automotive telemetry data exhibits slow drifts and fast spikes, often within the same sequence, making reliable anomaly detection challenging. Standard reconstruction-based methods, including sequence variational autoencoders (VAEs), use a single latent process and therefore mix heterogeneous time scales, which can smooth out spikes or inflate variances and weaken anomaly separation. In this paper, we present STREAM-VAE, a variational autoencoder for anomaly detection in automotive telemetry time-series data. Our model uses a dual-path encoder to separate slow drift and fast spike signal dynamics, and a decoder that represents transient deviations separately from the normal operating pattern. STREAM-VAE is designed for deployment, producing stable anomaly scores across operating modes for both in-vehicle monitors and backend fleet analytics. Experiments on an automotive telemetry dataset and the public SMD benchmark show that explicitly separating drift and spike dynamics improves robustness compared to strong forecasting, attention, graph, and VAE baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：STREAM-VAE：用于车载遥测异常检测的双路径路由机制
汽车遥测数据常同时包含缓慢漂移和快速尖峰，使可靠异常检测颇具挑战。标准的基于重构的方法（如序列变分自编码器VAE）使用单一潜在过程，混合了异质时间尺度，可能导致尖峰被平滑或方差被放大，从而削弱异常分离能力。 本文提出STREAM-VAE，一种面向汽车遥测时序数据的异常检测变分自编码器。模型采用双路径编码器分离缓慢漂移与快速尖峰信号动态，并通过解码器将瞬态偏差与正常运行模式独立表示。STREAM-VAE专为部署设计，可为车载监控与后端车队分析提供稳定异常评分。 在汽车遥测数据集及公开SMD基准测试中，实验表明显式分离漂移与尖峰动态相比强预测、注意力、图神经网络及VAE基线方法更具鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of detecting anomalies in automotive telemetry data that simultaneously contains slow drifts and fast spikes, the authors propose STREAM-VAE, a variational autoencoder that employs a dual-path encoder to separately model these heterogeneous time scales. The model’s decoder then reconstructs normal operating patterns apart from transient deviations, enabling stable anomaly scoring across varying vehicle conditions. Experiments on automotive telemetry and the SMD benchmark demonstrate that STREAM-VAE outperforms strong baselines including forecasting, attention, graph, and standard VAE models in anomaly detection robustness.
为了解决汽车遥测数据中同时存在缓慢漂移和快速突变导致的异常检测难题，本文提出STREAM-VAE，一种采用双路径编码器分别建模慢速漂移与快速突变的变分自编码器。解码器将正常运行模式与瞬态偏差分离，从而在不同车辆工况下提供稳定的异常评分。在汽车遥测数据和SMD基准测试中，该方法显著优于包括预测、注意力、图神经网络和标准VAE在内的多个强基线模型，展现出更强的异常检测鲁棒性。
Continuous Diffusion Transformers for Designing Synthetic Regulatory Elements
Authors: Jonathan Liu, Kia Ghods
First: 2026-03-11T15:30:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-11T15:30:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a parameter-efficient Diffusion Transformer (DiT) for generating 200bp cell-type-specific regulatory DNA sequences. By replacing the U-Net backbone of DNA-Diffusion with a transformer denoiser equipped with a 2D CNN input encoder, our model matches the U-Net's best validation loss in 13 epochs (60$\times$ fewer) and converges 39% lower, while reducing memorization from 5.3% to 1.7% of generated sequences aligning to training data via BLAT. Ablations show the CNN encoder is essential: without it, validation loss increases 70% regardless of positional embedding choice. We further apply DDPO finetuning using Enformer as a reward model, achieving a 38$\times$ improvement in predicted regulatory activity. Cross-validation against DRAKES on an independent prediction task confirms that improvements reflect genuine regulatory signal rather than reward model overfitting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于设计合成调控元件的连续扩散变换器
我们提出了一种参数高效的扩散变换器（DiT），用于生成200bp的细胞类型特异性调控DNA序列。通过将DNA-Diffusion的U-Net主干替换为配备2D CNN输入编码器的变换器去噪器，我们的模型在13个周期内（减少60倍）达到与U-Net相同的最佳验证损失，收敛值降低39%，同时将通过BLAT比对训练数据的生成序列记忆率从5.3%降至1.7%。消融实验表明CNN编码器至关重要：无论选择何种位置编码，移除它都会使验证损失增加70%。我们进一步使用Enformer作为奖励模型进行DDPO微调，预测调控活性提升38倍。在独立预测任务上，通过与DRAKES的交叉验证确认，性能提升源于真实的调控信号，而非奖励模型过拟合。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and accurate generation of cell-type-specific regulatory DNA sequences, the authors propose a parameter-efficient Diffusion Transformer (DiT) that replaces the U-Net backbone in DNA-Diffusion with a transformer denoiser and a 2D CNN input encoder. The model achieves comparable validation loss to U-Net in just 13 epochs (60 times faster) and converges with 39% lower loss while reducing memorization from 5.3% to 1.7%. Ablation studies confirm the CNN encoder’s necessity, as removing it increases validation loss by 70%. Further finetuning with DDPO using Enformer as a reward model boosts predicted regulatory activity by 38 times, and cross-validation on DRAKES verifies that performance gains stem from genuine regulatory signal, not reward model overfitting.
为高效生成细胞类型特异性的调控DNA序列，作者提出一种参数高效的扩散Transformer（DiT），用Transformer去噪器和2D CNN输入编码器替代DNA-Diffusion中的U-Net骨干结构。该模型仅需13个周期（快60倍）即可达到U-Net的最佳验证损失，收敛损失低39%，记忆化率从5.3%降至1.7%。消融实验表明CNN编码器不可或缺，移除后验证损失上升70%。进一步使用Enformer作为奖励模型进行DDPO微调，预测调控活性提升38倍，且在DRAKES上的交叉验证证实性能提升源于真实的调控信号，而非奖励模型过拟合。
Toward Closed-loop Molecular Discovery via Language Model, Property Alignment and Strategic Search
Authors: Junkai Ji, Zhangfan Yang, Dong Xu, Ruibin Bai, Jianqiang Li, Tingjun Hou, Zexuan Zhu
First: 2025-12-10T11:59:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-11T15:30:03+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Drug discovery is a time-consuming and expensive process, with traditional high-throughput and docking-based virtual screening hampered by low success rates and limited scalability. Recent advances in generative modelling, including autoregressive, diffusion, and flow-based approaches, have enabled de novo ligand design beyond the limits of enumerative screening. Yet these models often suffer from inadequate generalization, limited interpretability, and an overemphasis on binding affinity at the expense of key pharmacological properties, thereby restricting their translational utility. Here we present Trio, a molecular generation framework integrating fragment-based molecular language modeling, reinforcement learning, and Monte Carlo tree search, for effective and interpretable closed-loop targeted molecular design. Through the three key components, Trio enables context-aware fragment assembly, enforces physicochemical and synthetic feasibility, and guides a balanced search between the exploration of novel chemotypes and the exploitation of promising intermediates within protein binding pockets. Experimental results show that Trio reliably achieves chemically valid and pharmacologically enhanced ligands, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches with improved binding affinity (+7.85%), drug-likeness (+11.10%) and synthetic accessibility (+12.05%), while expanding molecular diversity more than fourfold. By combining generalization, plausibility, and interpretability, Trio establishes a closed-loop generative paradigm that redefines how chemical space can be navigated, offering a transformative foundation for the next era of AI-driven drug discovery.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过语言模型、性质对齐与策略搜索实现闭环分子发现
药物发现是一个耗时且昂贵的过程，传统的高通量和对接虚拟筛选受限于成功率低和可扩展性差。近年来，生成模型（包括自回归、扩散和基于流的方法）的进步使得超越枚举筛选的全新配体设计成为可能。然而，这些模型常存在泛化能力不足、可解释性有限，且过度关注结合亲和力而忽视关键药理性质的问题，从而限制了其转化应用。本文提出Trio，一个整合基于片段的分子语言建模、强化学习和蒙特卡洛树搜索的分子生成框架，用于高效且可解释的闭环靶向分子设计。通过三大核心组件，Trio实现上下文感知的片段组装，强制满足理化性质与合成可行性，并在探索新颖化学类型与利用蛋白结合口袋内有前景的中间体之间实现平衡搜索。实验结果表明，Trio能可靠生成化学上合理且药理性质增强的配体，相比现有最先进方法，结合亲和力提升7.85%，类药性提升11.10%，合成可及性提升12.05%，同时分子多样性扩大四倍以上。通过结合泛化能力、合理性与可解释性，Trio建立了一种闭环生成范式，重新定义了化学空间的探索方式，为AI驱动药物发现的下一个时代奠定变革性基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and limited scalability of traditional drug discovery methods, this work introduces Trio, a closed-loop molecular design framework that combines fragment-based language modeling, reinforcement learning, and Monte Carlo tree search to generate novel ligands with balanced pharmacological properties. Trio enables context-aware fragment assembly, enforces synthetic and physicochemical feasibility, and strategically balances exploration of new chemotypes with exploitation of promising binding pocket intermediates. Experimental results demonstrate that Trio produces chemically valid molecules with significantly improved binding affinity, drug-likeness, and synthetic accessibility compared to state-of-the-art methods, while also expanding molecular diversity by over fourfold.
为克服传统药物发现效率低和现有生成模型在平衡结合亲和力与药理性质方面的不足，Trio提出一种结合片段语言建模、强化学习与蒙特卡洛树搜索的闭环分子设计框架。该方法支持上下文感知的分子组装，强制确保化学与合成可行性，并在探索新化学结构与利用有潜力的结合口袋中间体之间实现策略性平衡。实验表明，Trio生成的分子在结合亲和力、类药性与合成可及性方面均优于现有方法，同时显著提升分子多样性。
Chain-of-Thought Compression Should Not Be Blind: V-Skip for Efficient Multimodal Reasoning via Dual-Path Anchoring
Authors: Dongxu Zhang, Yiding Sun, Cheng Tan, Wenbiao Yan, Ning Yang, Jihua Zhu, Haijun Zhang
First: 2026-01-20T11:45:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-11T15:28:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning significantly enhances the performance of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), its autoregressive nature incurs prohibitive latency constraints. Current efforts to mitigate this via token compression often fail by blindly applying text-centric metrics to multimodal contexts. We identify a critical failure mode termed Visual Amnesia, where linguistically redundant tokens are erroneously pruned, leading to hallucinations. To address this, we introduce V-Skip that reformulates token pruning as a Visual-Anchored Information Bottleneck (VA-IB) optimization problem. V-Skip employs a dual-path gating mechanism that weighs token importance through both linguistic surprisal and cross-modal attention flow, effectively rescuing visually salient anchors. Extensive experiments on Qwen2-VL and Llama-3.2 families demonstrate that V-Skip achieves a $2.9\times$ speedup with negligible accuracy loss. Specifically, it preserves fine-grained visual details, outperforming other baselines over 30\% on the DocVQA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：思维链压缩不应盲目：通过双路径锚定实现高效多模态推理的V-Skip
尽管思维链（CoT）推理显著提升了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的性能，但其自回归特性导致严重的延迟限制。当前通过令牌压缩缓解该问题的努力往往失败，因为盲目将文本中心的度量应用于多模态上下文。我们识别出一种关键失败模式，称为视觉遗忘，即语言冗余令牌被错误剪枝，导致幻觉。为解决此问题，我们引入V-Skip，将令牌剪枝重新表述为视觉锚定信息瓶颈（VA-IB）优化问题。V-Skip采用双路径门控机制，通过语言惊讶度和跨模态注意力流共同权衡令牌重要性，有效保留视觉显著锚点。在Qwen2-VL和Llama-3.2系列模型上的大量实验表明，V-Skip实现2.9倍加速，且准确率损失微乎其微。具体而言，它保留了细粒度视觉细节，在DocVQA上比其他基线方法高出30%以上。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high latency of autoregressive Chain-of-Thought reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models, the paper proposes V-Skip to compress tokens without sacrificing performance. It addresses the failure of existing methods that blindly apply text-centric metrics by introducing a Visual-Anchored Information Bottleneck framework with a dual-path gating mechanism that jointly evaluates token importance via linguistic surprisal and cross-modal attention. Experiments on Qwen2-VL and Llama-3.2 models show V-Skip achieves a 2.9x speedup with minimal accuracy loss, preserving fine-grained visual details and outperforming baselines by over 30% on DocVQA.
为解决多模态大语言模型中链式思维推理带来的高延迟问题，本文提出V-Skip方法，通过视觉锚定信息瓶颈框架对token进行压缩。该方法采用双路径门控机制，结合语言惊讶度与跨模态注意力流评估token重要性，避免盲目压缩导致的视觉信息丢失。在Qwen2-VL和Llama-3.2模型上的实验表明，V-Skip实现2.9倍加速，精度损失极小，并在DocVQA任务上比基线方法高出30%以上，有效保留了细粒度视觉细节。
LAtte: Hyperbolic Lorentz Attention for Cross-Subject EEG Classification
Authors: Johannes Burchert, Ahmad Bdeir, Tom Hanika, Lars Schmidt-Thieme, Niels Landwehr
First: 2026-03-11T15:27:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-11T15:27:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Electroencephalogram (EEG) classification is critical for applications ranging from medical diagnostics to brain-computer interfaces, yet it remains challenging due to the inherently low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) and high inter-subject variability. To address these issues, we propose LAtte, a novel framework that integrates a Lorentz Attention Module with an InceptionTime-based encoder to enable robust and generalizable EEG classification. Unlike prior work, which evaluates primarily on single-subject performance, LAtte focuses on cross-subject training. First, we learn a shared baseline signal across all subjects using pretraining tasks to capture common underlying patterns. Then, we utilize novel Lorentz low-rank adapters to learn subject-specific embeddings that model individual differences. This allows us to learn a shared model that performs robustly across subjects, and can be subsequently finetuned for individual subjects or used to generalize to unseen subjects. We evaluate LAtte on three well-established EEG datasets, achieving a substantial improvement in performance over current state-of-the-art methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LAtte：用于跨受试者EEG分类的双曲洛伦兹注意力机制
脑电图（EEG）分类在医学诊断和脑机接口等应用中至关重要，但由于固有的信噪比（SNR）低和受试者间高度变异性，仍具挑战性。为解决这些问题，我们提出LAtte框架，结合洛伦兹注意力模块与InceptionTime编码器，实现稳健且可泛化的EEG分类。与以往主要评估单受试者性能的工作不同，LAtte聚焦于跨受试者训练：首先，通过预训练任务学习所有受试者共享的基线信号，以捕捉共同潜在模式；随后，利用新颖的洛伦兹低秩适配器学习受试者特异性嵌入，建模个体差异。这使我们能够学习一个在不同受试者间表现稳健的共享模型，并可进一步微调用于个体受试者，或泛化至未见受试者。我们在三个经典EEG数据集上评估LAtte，显著优于当前最先进方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of low signal-to-noise ratio and inter-subject variability in EEG classification, the paper introduces LAtte, a framework combining a Lorentz Attention Module with an InceptionTime encoder to enable cross-subject generalization. The method first pretrains a shared baseline signal across subjects to capture common patterns, then employs Lorentz low-rank adapters to learn subject-specific embeddings that model individual differences. Evaluated on three EEG datasets, LAtte demonstrates significant performance gains over existing state-of-the-art methods, showing robustness across subjects and strong generalization capability.
为应对EEG分类中信号噪声比低和跨受试者差异大的挑战，本文提出LAtte框架，结合Lorentz注意力模块与InceptionTime编码器以实现跨受试者泛化。该方法首先通过预训练任务学习所有受试者共享的基线信号以捕捉共性模式，再利用Lorentz低秩适配器学习反映个体差异的受试者特异性嵌入。在三个EEG数据集上的实验表明，LAtte在跨受试者分类任务中显著优于当前最先进方法。
Ego: Embedding-Guided Personalization of Vision-Language Models
Authors: Soroush Seifi, Simon Gardier, Vaggelis Dorovatas, Daniel Olmeda Reino, Rahaf Aljundi
Venue: CVPR
First: 2026-03-10T15:10:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-11T15:26:01+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the IEEE/CVF Conference on Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (CVPR) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI assistants that support humans in daily life are becoming increasingly feasible, driven by the rapid advancements in multimodal language models. A key challenge lies in overcoming the generic nature of these models to deliver personalized experiences. Existing approaches to personalizing large vision language models often rely on additional training stages, which limit generality and scalability, or on engineered pipelines with external pre-trained modules, which hinder deployment efficiency. In this work, we propose an efficient personalization method that leverages the model's inherent ability to capture personalized concepts. Specifically, we extract visual tokens that predominantly represent the target concept by utilizing the model's internal attention mechanisms. These tokens serve as a memory of that specific concept, enabling the model to recall and describe it when it appears in test images. We conduct a comprehensive and unified evaluation of our approach and SOTA methods across various personalization settings including single-concept, multi-concept, and video personalization, demonstrating strong performance gains with minimal personalization overhead.
Summary / 总结
AI assistants that support humans in daily life are becoming increasingly feasible, driven by the rapid advancements in multimodal language models.
RL-Augmented MPC for Non-Gaited Legged and Hybrid Locomotion
Authors: Andrea Patrizi, Carlo Rizzardo, Arturo Laurenzi, Francesco Ruscelli, Luca Rossini, Nikos G. Tsagarakis
First: 2026-03-11T15:24:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-11T15:24:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We propose a contact-explicit hierarchical architecture coupling Reinforcement Learning (RL) and Model Predictive Control (MPC), where a high-level RL agent provides gait and navigation commands to a low-level locomotion MPC. This offloads the combinatorial burden of contact timing from the MPC by learning acyclic gaits through trial and error in simulation. We show that only a minimal set of rewards and limited tuning are required to obtain effective policies. We validate the architecture in simulation across robotic platforms spanning 50 kg to 120 kg and different MPC implementations, observing the emergence of acyclic gaits and timing adaptations in flat-terrain legged and hybrid locomotion, and further demonstrating extensibility to non-flat terrains. Across all platforms, we achieve zero-shot sim-to-sim transfer without domain randomization, and we further demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real transfer without domain randomization on Centauro, our 120 kg wheeled-legged humanoid robot. We make our software framework and evaluation results publicly available at https://github.com/AndrePatri/AugMPC.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于强化学习增强的模型预测控制用于非步态式腿式与混合式移动
我们提出一种显式接触的分层架构，将强化学习（RL）与模型预测控制（MPC）相结合，其中高层RL代理向低层运动MPC提供步态与导航指令。该架构通过在仿真环境中通过试错学习无环步态，将接触时机的组合负担从MPC中卸载。我们表明，仅需少量奖励和有限调参即可获得有效策略。我们在涵盖50kg至120kg不同机器人平台及多种MPC实现的仿真中验证了该架构，观察到在平坦地形下腿式与混合式移动中无环步态与时机适应的涌现，并进一步证明其可扩展至非平坦地形。所有平台均实现无域随机化零样本仿真到仿真迁移，且在我们的120kg轮腿式人形机器人Centauro上实现无域随机化零样本仿真到真实世界迁移。我们公开了软件框架与评估结果，详见https://github.com/AndrePatri/AugMPC。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational complexity of contact timing in legged locomotion, this work proposes a hierarchical RL-MPC framework where an RL agent generates gait and navigation commands while a low-level MPC handles execution. The RL component learns acyclic gaits through simulation-based trial and error, offloading contact sequencing from the MPC and requiring minimal reward design and tuning. Experiments across 50–120 kg robotic platforms show the system autonomously discovers acyclic gaits and adapts timing for flat-terrain legged and hybrid locomotion, with zero-shot transfer from simulation to simulation and to real-world deployment on the Centauro robot without domain randomization.
为降低腿式机器人运动中接触点规划的计算负担，该研究提出一种结合强化学习与模型预测控制的分层架构：高层RL代理生成步态与导航指令，底层MPC负责执行。RL模块通过仿真中的试错学习无环步态，将接触时序规划任务卸载给MPC，且仅需少量奖励设计与调参。实验在50至120公斤级机器人平台上验证，系统自主发现无环步态并适应平坦地形下的腿式与混合式运动，且在无领域随机化条件下实现仿真到仿真及仿真到真实机器人的零样本迁移，成功部署于Centauro人形机器人。
